Native boundary-length hexagon experiment

Finite common-scale integer-length 120-degree hexagons; native full-edge honeycomb CSP; finite failures are scoped to this model; periodic absence is bounded; symbolic disks are not physical circles or certified tile-corona counts.

All six angles are 120°. This family overlaps earlier repository experiments; no novelty claim is made. Every native length uses one shared scale.

Screen bounds: HNF index ≤ 4, symbolic disk radii [1, 2, 4].
